tbmm-2020-12-16sit02spe0105par0062	Members of the Parliament, the 161st Article of the Constitution, which regulates the budget and final account, is read and voted on without discussion. The issue of the Turkish public financing burden of city hospitals and other public-private cooperation projects and for other reasons guarantees has been one of the most controversial issues during the meeting of the 2021 Central Management Budget Law Proposal in the Commission. Contracts on long-term public-private co-operation projects have taken down the policy preferences of many governments in the field of both health and public finances, and have almost completely eliminated the opportunities for preferentialisation. Public investments are based on budget opportunities in almost every country in the world. It is considered to be contrary to financial discipline and budget principles for public investments to be done in non-budget ways. In our country, the first public-private cooperation models in energy and transport areas are seen to be increasingly common during the AKP, including education and health, and its financial portrait has been raised. Meanwhile, the use of the public-private cooperation model, which has been implemented during the AKP period, has been seen to have been moved far beyond the preferences of advanced technology or services, due to the exceptions of the relevant law, and is used as a means of funding for similar capital groups. We all know that the economy of Turkey is giving budget deficits. In this framework, instead of the KCI model of infrastructure investments, it will naturally increase the budget deficit and the treasure will be more in debt. However, in particular in terms of foreign debt, it would be appropriate to compare the burden of borrowing the treasure personally and borrowing it by the private sector under the KCI model to the country's economy. This issue is of great importance, given that the treasury has the means to borrow with lower interest and longer time. On the other hand, investments with the public-special cooperation model create a conditional obligation to the future. This conditional obligation has been established with the opportunity to guarantee a treasure for investments with the KCI model. In other words, the private sector external debt will be loaded by the treasury when certain conditions occur in investments with the KCI model, during the business or rent period. Clearly, this would pose a serious financial risk to the Turkish financial system. In such a case, the risk of the treasure will rise, as the treasure will pay off the debt and the public debt will increase. To ensure that the burden of the city hospitals and other public-private cooperation projects and the treasury guarantees for other reasons will be publicly known for the five years following the budget year, this proposal is made to add the following years to the central budget laws. Large-scale companies benefit from tax exemption due to the fact that their location is in the rural area. The exclusion of large-scale firms with a specific turnover or other criteria will prevent unfair competition from being introduced by Law 6360. Another issue is the confusion of authority and duty. The adoption of the city's districts of Turkey's southeastern province of Turkey's southeastern province of Turkey's southeastern part of Turkey's southeastern province of Turkey's southeastern province of Turkey, has led to a conflict of duty and authority in implementing its municipalities with its municipalities. Defence industry projects are activities that are not used in civilian sectors to create high-tech products, which are high-priced and extended for a long time. These activities, which have been declared a priority by the Defense Industry Undersecretary, are the system compatibility of aircraft, warships and armored vehicles; networks, information, satellite, sensor systems; electronic warfare, missile and guidance control. The Turkish defence industry, which was founded in the 19th century in the Ottoman Empire, began to develop with various organizations and initiatives in the period leading to the Second World War. After World War II, however, close labor troops formed with the United States, and the aid this country has done to Turkey, have been in the way of the development of our defence industry. The arms embargo of the United States after Operation Peace in Cyprus in 1974 showed Turkey the need for a national defence industry and began to take steps in that direction.
